SkillObservation(content='Base directory for this skill: /workspace/app-bu7wnuu44l4x/.skills/supabase-server\n\n# Supabase Backend\n\nApplies to all frontend stacks (Web / MiniProgram / App). Covers Supabase server-side Schema, RLS, Edge Functions, Storage, Realtime, and Auth configuration.\n\n## Initial Setup\n\n- Use `supabase_init` to initialize the database or start it from a paused state\n\n## Schema Design\n\n- Minimize tables and columns; merge functionalities into single tables when possible\n- Set database-level default values for required fields\n  - Example: `owner_id uuid not null default auth.uid()`\n  - Example: `order_number text UNIQUE NOT NULL` — use a trigger bound to insert logic to auto-generate the order number\n- For multi-table relations, define them clearly to avoid missing link fields\n- ENUM values require explicit casts\n- **NEVER interact with `supabase/migrations/` directory** (including ls, cat, view)\n- Always apply changes through incremental migrations\n- Verify database state by querying table structure using `supabase_execute_sql`\n- Try to combine tasks and execute all SQL in a single run\n- Always consider performance and speed optimization\n- Disallow SQL statements with silent or ambiguous success semantics (e.g., `IF NOT EXISTS`, `IF EXISTS`) — all operations must fail explicitly when schema or data deviates from expectations\n\n## Data Insertion\n\n**Must-Add:**\n- System-required data (e.g., categories, form dropdown options)\n- **For image URLs**: Use `image_search` to get real URLs — **NEVER** use placeholder URLs like `https://placeholder-image.jpg`\n- Add initial seed data (INSERT INTO) in the migration SQL, using `image_search` for real image URLs\n\n**Allowed:**\n- Homepage display data (e.g., showcases, course previews, products)\n- Demo data (e.g., sample exam sets)\n\n**Forbidden:**\n- User data\n- User activity history (e.g., lottery, orders)\n- Real personal names or any privacy-related info\n\n## SQL Modification\n\n- If you have full context in memory, prioritize using it directly\n- If unsure about current data structure, query it first; do not rely on migration files\n- Under no circumstances should you view or modify migration files\n- If frontend requires a feature without a corresponding table, policy, or edge function, you may add it afterward\n- When modifying business data (non-DDL, policy, RPC, etc.), use `supabase_execute_sql`\n- Before writing api.ts, read the corresponding `supabase/migrations/*.sql` to confirm NOT NULL constraints for every column — context compression may lose field details; migrations are the ground truth\n\n## Security Policies (RLS)\n\n### Design Principles\n\n- **Every table MUST have RLS enabled** — no exceptions, regardless of whether login auth exists\n- **Full coverage per table**: Explicitly define policies for every combination of SELECT/INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE × {anon, authenticated, admin}. **Never leave any blank** — missing policies silently return empty data or 0 affected rows\n  - Per-table review checklist: anon × {S/I/U/D}, authenticated × {S/I/U/D}, admin × {S/I/U/D}\n  - Anticipate future features: add corresponding constraints now for role×operation combinations that may be needed later\n- **INSERT always requires a matching SELECT**: Any role granted INSERT permission MUST also have a SELECT policy defined\n  - Example: If a policy "allows anyone to submit", add one so "submitters can view their own submissions"\n- Frontend and backend permissions must be aligned\n  - No permission, no interaction\n  - All submit-capable forms must have explicit, role-based permissions\n- PostgREST silently returns 0 affected rows (no error) when UPDATE/DELETE does not match RLS — writes appear to succeed but nothing changed. Tables the frontend needs to mutate must have explicit UPDATE/DELETE policies defined\n- Clear role boundaries\n  - Admins must have full read/write access\n  - Roles must have complete and secure permissions defined for every possible interaction scenario\n- All internal checks must use `SECURITY DEFINER`\n\n### Implementation Notes\n\n- RLS should only depend on **static columns or secure functions**\n- **Policy self-loop**: A policy\'s USING clause must not query the same table it protects — use a `SECURITY DEFINER` helper function instead (it bypasses RLS when querying the table)\n- Use public views to expose read-only access to selected fields of high-privilege tables\n\n## Storage Buckets\n\n- Create for image/file uploads only\n- **CRITICAL**: NEVER store images/videos as Base64 in database\n- ALL image/video assets MUST be uploaded to Supabase Storage\n- When third-party APIs return image/video URLs, transfer to Storage in Edge Function\n- buckets must be created in migration SQL via `supabase_apply_migration` — **do not** create them dynamically inside Edge Functions\n- Frontend validation: 1MB limit, snake_case filenames\n\n\n## Edge Functions\n\n### When to Use\n\n- **MANDATORY for third-party API calls**: ALL external API integrations (payment, weather, maps, etc.) MUST be implemented through Edge Functions\n- **STRICTLY FORBIDDEN**: Direct third-party API calls from client-side code (fetch/axios in frontend components)\n- Server-side secrets and service-role operations\n- Sensitive payment logic requiring secure environment\n\n### Authoring\n\n- Create files under `supabase/functions/<function_name>/`\n- **CRITICAL**: Use `supabase_deploy_edge_function` to deploy after modifying or creating Edge Function files\n- Put cross-domain content in the `_shared/` directory\n- No URL imports — only npm and JSR\n- Do not add secrets unless explicitly required by API documentation\n- Handle CORS properly\n- `supabase.sql` forbidden in Edge Functions\n- `supabase.raw` forbidden in Edge Functions — use `.rpc()` for atomic operations\n- Response Content-Type must be chosen only from: `[\'application/json\', \'application/octet-stream\', \'text/event-stream\', \'multipart/form-data\']`\n- Make proper use of optimistic locking to ensure that subsequent settlement operations in the state flow are executed only once, even if called multiple times\n\n\n## Supabase Auth\n\n- Your knowledge may be outdated. **MUST** call `skill_action(skill="login")` to refresh with the latest information\n- Any form of third-party login (OAuth or SSO) must use the login skill as the knowledge basis\n\n## Supabase Realtime (low-frequency feature)\n\nWhen realtime capability is genuinely needed, read `references/realtime.md` — it covers server-side publication switches, per-stack client subscription patterns, and RN/weapp limitations.\n\n**Multi-identity scenarios**: Identity selection / role assignment logic **must NOT** be placed on the frontend — it **MUST** be encapsulated in an Edge Function.', observation=<ObservationType.SKILL: 'skill'>, skill='supabase-server', success=True, data={'skill_path': '/workspace/miaoda_skills/prompts/system/supabase-server/SKILL.md', 'scripts_path': None})

SSE 流式响应（心跳 + 结果） | 用 `fetch` + `getReader()` 消费 SSE 流，收到 `type: "result"` 事件后解析 |\n| Web（编辑图片） | SSE 流式响应（心跳 + 结果） | 用 `fetch` + `getReader()` 消费 SSE 流，收到 `type: "result"` 事件后解析 |\n| MiniProgram | JSON（含 Base64） | 解析 JSON，写临时文件后用 `<image>` 组件展示 |\n\n详细参数说明、代码示例及两平台完整实现见：\n- `references/image-generations-api.md` — 创建图片接口\n- `references/image-edits-api.md` — 编辑图片接口\n\n---\n\n## 使用前决策\n\n调用本工具前，先判断场景是否真的需要 AI 生成：\n\n| 场景 | 推荐方案 |\n|------|---------|\n| 根据文字描述生成全新图片 | ✅ 本工具（文生图） |\n| 上传图片 + 提示词做风格转换或内容编辑 | ✅ 本工具（图生图） |\n| 多张图片融合 / 背景替换 / 海报合成 | ✅ 本工具（多图编辑） |\n| 在图片上叠加文字 / 水印 | ❌ 改用 Pillow（速度快、可离线） |\n| 裁剪、缩放、格式转换、像素级操作 | ❌ 改用 Pillow |\n| 图片内容审核 / 质量评分 | ❌ 改用视觉模型直接分析，无需生成 |\n\n---\n\n## Prompt 编写规范\n\n底层模型（GPT-Image-2）对英文提示词的理解和图像质量通常优于中文，请优先将用户需求改写为英文后再提交 API。\n\n**写作原则：**\n- 使用描述句，直接描述目标画面，而非告诉模型"帮我生成……"\n- 具体优于抽象：`"a ginger cat sitting in a sunlit garden"` 好于 `"可爱的猫"`\n- 避免否定词：不写 `"no background"`，改写 `"isolated on pure white background"`\n- 末尾加质量修饰词提升细节：`high quality`, `detailed`, `8k`, `photorealistic`\n\n**文生图模板：**\n\n```\n[Subject], [Action/Pose/State], [Scene/Environment], [Lighting], [Style], [Quality]\n```\n\n示例：\n```\nA golden retriever puppy, sitting and looking up curiously, in a cozy living room with warm afternoon lighting, watercolor illustration style, high quality, detailed\n```\n\n**图生图 / 多图编辑额外建议：**\n- 先描述希望**保留**的内容，再描述希望**改变**的内容\n- 风格迁移时明确目标风格，例如 `"convert to anime style"` 或 `"oil painting style"`\n- 多图融合时说明图片之间的关系，例如 `"use image 1 as background, place the product from image 2 in the center"`\n\n---\n\n## 生成期用法（Agent 直接调用）\n\n> **在调用 API 之前，先将用户需求翻译/改写为英文提示词**，GPT-Image-2 模型对英文输入的图像质量明显优于中文。\n\n两个接口均为同步调用，直接返回 Base64 编码图片数据，不含 URL。获得响应后必须立即将 Base64 解码保存为图片文件。\n\n```typescript\nconst apiKey = process.env["INTEGRATIONS_API_KEY"]!;\n\ninterface CreateImageResult {\n  created: number;\n  data: Array<{\n    b64_json: string;\n    revised_prompt: string;\n  }>;\n  background: string;\n  output_format: string;\n  quality: string;\n  size: string;\n  model: string;\n}\n\n/** 创建图片（文生图） */\nasync function createImage(\n  prompt: string,\n  size?: string,\n  n?: number\n): Promise<CreateImageResult> {\n  const response = await fetch(\n    "http://app-bu7wnuu44l4x-api-wLNdpny6ZpVa-gateway.appmiaoda.com/v1/images/generations",\n    {\n      method: "POST",\n      headers: {\n        "Content-Type": "application/json",\n        "X-Gateway-Authorization": `Bearer ${apiKey}`,\n      },\n      body: JSON.stringify({\n        model: "gpt-image-2",\n        prompt,\n        size,\n        n,\n      }),\n    }\n  );\n  if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP error: ${response.status}`);\n  const json = await response.json();\n  if (json.error) throw new Error(`API error: ${JSON.stringify(json.error)}`);\n  return json;\n}\n\ninterface EditImageResult {\n  created: number;\n  data: Array<{\n    b64_json: string;\n    revised_prompt: string;\n  }>;\n  background: string;\n  output_format: string;\n  quality: string;\n  size: string;\n  model: string;\n  usage?: {\n    input_tokens: number;\n    input_tokens_details: { image_tokens: number; text_tokens: number };\n    output_tokens: number;\n    output_tokens_details: { image_tokens: number; text_tokens: number };\n    total_tokens: number;\n  };\n}\n\n/** 编辑图片（多图融合/编辑） */\nasync function editImage(\n  prompt: string,\n  images: File[],\n  size?: string,\n  n?: number\n): Promise<EditImageResult> {\n  const formData = new FormData();\n  formData.append("model", "gpt-image-2");\n  formData.append("prompt", prompt);\n  if (size) formData.append("size", size);\n  if (n) formData.append("n", String(n));\n  images.forEach((file, index) => {\n    formData.append(`image[${index}]`, file);\n  });\n\n  const response = await fetch(\n    "http://app-bu7wnuu44l4x-api-wLNdpny6ZpVa-gateway.appmiaoda.com/v1/images/edits",\n    {\n      method: "POST",\n      headers: {\n        "X-Gateway-Authorization": `Bearer ${apiKey}`,\n      },\n      body: formData,\n    }\n  );\n  if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP error: ${response.status}`);\n  const json = await response.json();\n  if (json.error) throw new Error(`API error: ${JSON.stringify(json.error)}`);\n  return json;\n}\n```\n\n**生成期文件保存（必须执行）：**\n\n两个接口均返回 Base64 编码图片，数据仅存在于当次响应中。获得 Base64 后，**必须立即使用 Bash 工具将其解码并保存到本地**，以便用户查看结果。\n\n```bash\necho "<base64_data>" | base64 -d > <本地路径>.png\n```\n\n**完整生成期工作流（含保存步骤）：**\n\n1. 根据用户需求选择对应接口（`createImage` 或 `editImage`）\n2. 构造请求参数并调用接口获取响应\n3. 从 `json.data[0].b64_json` 提取 Base64 数据\n4. 使用 Bash 工具将 Base64 解码并保存：`echo "<b64_json>" | base64 -d > <本地路径>.png`\n5. 告知用户文件已保存到对应路径，同时展示 `revised_prompt`\n\n> **注意**：Base64 数据仅存在于当次响应中，必须及时保存，否则数据丢失。\n\n**空间位置描述（生成期 Prompt 增强）：**\n\n在提示词中加入空间位置词可显著提高构图准确性：\n\n| 位置关键词 | 说明 | 示例 |\n|-----------|------|------|\n| `centered` / `in the center` | 主体居中 | `"a red rose, centered, white background"` |\n| `in the top-left / bottom-right corner` | 角落定位 | `"logo in the top-left corner"` |\n| `in the foreground / background` | 前景/背景层次 | `"flowers in the foreground, mountains in the background"` |\n| `on the left side / right side` | 左右分布 | `"person on the left, product on the right"` |\n| `filling the entire frame` | 占满画面 | `"texture filling the entire frame"` |\n\n---\n\n## 生成后用法（应用内通过 Edge Function 调用）\n\n应用内通过 Edge Function 安全调用上游 API，密钥不暴露给前端。\n\n**安全合约：**\n- 前端只发送业务参数到 Edge Function，不接触 API Key\n- Edge Function 从 `Deno.env.get("INTEGRATIONS_API_KEY")` 读取密钥\n- 请求上游时注入 `X-Gateway-Authorization: Bearer ${apiKey}`\n- `429`（配额超限）和 `402`（余额不足）错误体原样透传给前端\n- 返回的 Base64 数据由前端接收并解码渲染\n\n**Edge Function 实现：**\n- `image-generations`：代理创建图片接口，处理 JSON 请求，解析 body 后强制注入 `body.model = "gpt-image-2"`，**采用 SSE 流式响应**，每 15 秒发送心跳事件防止中间路由超时断开连接，最终以 `type: "result"` 事件返回结果\n- `image-edits`：代理编辑图片接口，**必须用 `req.formData()` 解析请求体**（不能用 `req.arrayBuffer()` 直接透传），检查并注入 `model` 字段（`formData.set("model", "gpt-image-2")`），然后**采用 SSE 流式响应**，每 15 秒发送心跳事件防止中间路由超时断开连接，最终以 `type: "result"` 事件返回结果\n\n完整 Edge Function 代码和前端调用代码详见：\n- `references/image-generations-api.md`（创建图片的 Edge Function + 前端代码）\n- `references/image-edits-api.md`（编辑图片的 Edge Function + 前端代码）\n\n---\n\n## 参数说明\n\n### 创建图片核心参数\n\n| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |\n|------|------|------|------|\n| `model` | `string` | 是 | 固定值：`gpt-image-2` |\n| `prompt` | `string` | 是 | 图片生成描述词 |\n| `size` | `string` | 否 | 输出尺寸：`1024x1024`、`1536x1024`、`1024x1536`、`2848x1152` |\n| `n` | `integer` | 否 | 生成数量，默认 1 |\n\n### 编辑图片核心参数\n\n| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |\n|------|------|------|------|\n| `model` | `string` | 是 | 固定值：`gpt-image-2` |\n| `prompt` | `string` | 是 | 图片编辑描述词 |\n| `size` | `string` | 否 | 输出尺寸 |\n| `n` | `integer` | 否 | 输出数量，默认 1 |\n| `image[0]` | `file` | 是 | 主图片文件 |\n| `image[1]` | `file` | 否 | 附加图片文件 |\n| `image[2]` | `file` | 否 | 附加图片文件 |\n\n### 返回核心字段\n\n| 字段路径 | 类型 | 说明 |\n|----------|------|------|\n| `created` | `number` | 创建时间戳 |\n| `data` | `array` | 生成结果列表 |\n| `data[].b64_json` | `string` | Base64 编码图片内容 |\n| `data[].revised_prompt` | `string` | 模型自动优化后的提示词 |\n| `usage` | `object` | Token 消耗统计（仅编辑接口返回） |\n\n---\n\n## 注意事项\n\n- **密钥安全**：`INTEGRATIONS_API_KEY` 仅可在 Edge Function 服务端读取，严禁暴露到前端代码或客户端环境变量中。\n- **Base64 数据及时保存**：两个接口均返回 Base64 编码图片，数据仅存在于当次响应中。生成期必须在获得响应后立即使用 Bash `base64 -d` 保存为文件。\n- **文件上传限制**：编辑接口最多支持 3 张图片（`image[0]` 必填，`image[1]`、`image[2]` 可选），需确保图片格式和大小符合上游要求。\n- **model 参数必传**：`model` 字段（值为 `gpt-image-2`）是上游网关路由的必要字段，缺失会导致 403。对于 `image-generations`，Edge Function 在解析 JSON body 后注入 `body.model = "gpt-image-2"`；对于 `image-edits`，Edge Function 在解析 FormData 后检查并补充 `model` 字段。**即使前端忘记传递 model，Edge Function 也必须兜底注入**，确保上游请求中始终包含该字段。\n- **错误处理**：\n  - `429` — 配额已用尽\n  - `402` — 余额不足\n  - `400` — 请求参数错误\n  - `401` — 认证失败\n- **计费**：本插件未启用计费（`enable_billing: false`），但仍需确保 API Key 有效且配额充足。\n\n---\n\n## 失败兜底\n\n当生成质量不满足要求时，判断是否可以用 Pillow 完成：\n\n**可降级到 Pillow 的场景：**\n- 在图片上添加文字（标题、水印、标注）\n- 在图片上叠加 Logo 或图标\n- 裁剪、缩放、旋转、格式转换\n- 调整亮度/对比度/饱和度\n\n**Pillow 基础示例（生成期 Agent 可直接运行）：**\n\n```python\nfrom PIL import Image, ImageDraw, ImageFont\n\n# 在图片上叠加文字水印\ndef add_text_watermark(image_path: str, text: str, output_path: str) -> None:\n    img = Image.open(image_path).convert("RGBA")\n    draw = ImageDraw.Draw(img)\n    # 使用系统字体，或指定 .ttf 路径\n    try:\n        font = ImageFont.truetype("/System/Library/Fonts/PingFang.ttc", size=36)\n    except OSError:\n        font = ImageFont.load_default()\n    # 右下角绘制文字\n    w, h = img.size\n    bbox = draw.textbbox((0, 0), text, font=font)\n    tw, th = bbox[2] - bbox[0], bbox[3] - bbox[1]\n    draw.text((w - tw - 20, h - th - 20), text, fill=(255, 255, 255, 180), font=font)\n    img.convert("RGB").save(output_path)\n\nadd_text_watermark("input.png", "© 2024 My Brand", "output.png")\n```\n\n**决策流程：**\n1. 调用 AI 生成接口 → 如果成功且质量满意 → 完成\n2. 如果返回 `error` 或 HTTP `429`（配额超限）/`402`（余额不足）→ 向用户说明错误\n3. 如果用户需求属于纯像素操作 → 改用 Pillow\n4. 如果需求超出 Pillow 能力（如真正的语义编辑）→     23 /* ─── Markdown 块渲染（AI生成内容用） ─── */
    24 function renderMarkdownBlocks(text: string): React.ReactNode {
    25   const lines = text.split('\n');
    26   const elements: React.ReactNode[] = [];
    27   let i = 0;
    28   while (i < lines.length) {
    29     const line = lines[i];
    30     if (/^### /.test(line)) {
    31       elements.push(<h3 key={i} className="text-sm font-bold mt-3 mb-1 text-foreground">{renderInline(line.replace(/^### /, ''))}</h3>);
    32     } else if (/^## /.test(line)) {
    33       elements.push(<h2 key={i} className="text-sm font-semibold mt-3 mb-1.5 text-primary border-b border-border/40 pb-1">{renderInline(line.replace(/^## /, ''))}</h2>);
    34     } else if (/^# /.test(line)) {
    35       elements.push(<h1 key={i} className="text-base font-bold mt-3 mb-2 text-foreground">{renderInline(line.replace(/^# /, ''))}</h1>);
    36     } else if (/^\d+\. /.test(line.trimStart())) {
    37       const num = line.trimStart().match(/^(\d+)\./)?.[1];
    38       elements.push(
    39         <div key={i} className="flex gap-2 text-sm leading-relaxed">
    40           <span className="text-primary font-semibold shrink-0 min-w-[1.2rem]">{num}.</span>
    41           <span className="text-pretty">{renderInline(line.trimStart().replace(/^\d+\. /, ''))}</span>
    42         </div>
    43       );
    44     } else if (/^[-*•] /.test(line.trimStart())) {
    45       elements.push(
    46         <div key={i} className="flex gap-2 text-sm leading-relaxed">
    47           <span className="text-primary shrink-0 mt-1">•</span>
    48           <span className="text-pretty">{renderInline(line.trimStart().replace(/^[-*•] /, ''))}</span>
    49         </div>
    50       );
    51     } else if (/^```/.test(line)) {
    52       const lang = line.replace(/^```/, '').trim();
    53       const codeLines: string[] = [];
    54       i++;
    55       while (i < lines.length && !lines[i].startsWith('```')) { codeLines.push(lines[i]); i++; }
    56       elements.push(
    57         <div key={i} className="my-2 rounded-lg overflow-hidden border border-border/60">
    58           {lang && <div className="px-3 py-1 bg-muted text-[10px] font-mono text-muted-foreground border-b border-border/40">{lang}</div>}
    59           <pre className="px-3 py-2.5 text-xs font-mono leading-relaxed overflow-x-auto bg-muted/50 text-foreground"><code>{codeLines.join('\n')}</code></pre>
    60         </div>
    61       );
    62     } else if (/^---+$/.test(line.trim())) {
    63       elements.push(<hr key={i} className="my-3 border-border/40" />);
    64     } else if (/^> /.test(line)) {
    65       elements.push(<blockquote key={i} className="border-l-2 border-primary/50 pl-3 my-1.5 text-sm text-muted-foreground italic">{renderInline(line.replace(/^> /, ''))}</blockquote>);
    66     } else if (line.trim() === '') {
    67       if (elements.length > 0) elements.push(<div key={i} className="h-1.5" />);
    68     } else {
    69       elements.push(<p key={i} className="text-sm leading-relaxed text-pretty">{renderInline(line)}</p>);
    70     }
    71     i++;
    72   }
    73   return <div className="space-y-0.5 px-1">{elements}</div>;
    74 }

    65         const { data } = await supabase.from('chat_messages').select('*').eq('user_id', user.id).eq('session_type', 'portrait').eq('role', 'user');
    66         const count = Array.isArray(data) ? data.length : 0;
    67         const dims = portraitDimensions.slice(0, count).map(d => d.key);

    70           setIsComplete(true);
    71           await supabase.from('learning_portraits').upsert({
    72             user_id: user.id, is_complete: true,
    73             major_direction: { value: data?.[0]?.content || '' },
    74             knowledge_base: { value: data?.[1]?.content || '' },

[pptxgenjs.md](references/pptxgenjs.md) — API 语法与陷阱\n2. [design-system.md](references/design-system.md) — 配色、字体、风格\n3. **[pitfalls.md](references/pitfalls.md) — 布局陷阱与修复**（换行、溢出、流程图、时间轴）\n\n### 第一步 · 内容规划 / Step 1 — Content Planning\n\n写第一张幻灯片前，先回答这五个问题：\nBefore writing a single slide, answer these:\n\n1. 受众是谁？/ Who is the audience?\n2. 一句话结论是什么（金字塔原则）？/ What is the one-sentence conclusion (Pyramid Principle)?\n3. 支撑结论的 3-5 个理由？/ What are the 3-5 top-level supporting reasons?\n4. 观看后期望受众采取什么行动？/ Desired action after viewing?\n5. 语气：商务 / 教育 / 创意 / 技术？/ Tone: corporate / educational / creative / technical?\n\n如果用户没有指定，使用安全默认值（通用专业受众，12-15 张幻灯片，中性语气），并在备注中说明假设。\nIf unspecified, use safe defaults (professional audience, 12-15 slides, neutral tone) and note assumptions.\n\n**幻灯片结构规则 / Slide structure rule**: 每张幻灯片只传达 **1 个核心观点**。标题用断言句（"APAC 扩张推动营收增长 40%"），而不是话题标签（"营收"）。\nEach slide conveys exactly **1 core idea**. Headings are assertion sentences, not topic labels.\n\n**标题字数硬限制 / Title length hard limit**：中文标题 **≤ 15 字**，英文标题 **≤ 6 个单词**。超出则必须精简，不允许靠换行"塞进去"。标题换行意味着标题太长，应当重写而非调小字号。\n\n### 第二步 · 选择设计风格 / Step 2 — Pick Design Style\n\n查阅 [design-system.md](references/design-system.md)，选择：\nUse [design-system.md](references/design-system.md) to choose:\n\n- **配色方案**：匹配主题，不要默认用蓝色 / **Color palette**: match the topic, don\'t default to blue\n- **风格 recipe**：锐利 / 柔和 / 圆润 / 胶囊 · Sharp / Soft / Rounded / Pill\n- **字体组合**：如 Georgia + Calibri / **Font pairing** e.g. Georgia + Calibri\n\n### 第三步 · 规划幻灯片大纲 / Step 3 — Plan Slide Outline\n\n将每张幻灯片归为 [slide-types.md](references/slide-types.md) 中的 **5 种页面类型之一**：\nClassify every slide as exactly one of the **5 page types** in [slide-types.md](references/slide-types.md):\n\n- 封面 · Cover，目录 · TOC，章节分隔 · Section Divider，内容页 · Content，总结/结尾 · Summary/Closing\n\n相邻内容页**不能使用相同布局**，保持视觉多样性。\nEnsure visual variety — do NOT repeat the same layout across adjacent content slides.\n\n### 第四步 · 生成幻灯片 / Step 4 — Generate Slides\n\n使用 PptxGenJS，执行方式灵活，不强制一个幻灯片一个文件。\nUse PptxGenJS. Flexible execution — no need for one-file-per-slide unless using subagents.\n\n```javascript\nconst pptxgen = require("pptxgenjs");\nconst pres = new pptxgen();\npres.layout = \'LAYOUT_16x9\';\n\nconst theme = {\n  primary: "22223b",\n  secondary: "4a4e69",\n  accent: "9a8c98",\n  light: "c9ada7",\n  bg: "f2e9e4"\n};\n\n// 在此添加幻灯片 / Add slides here\n// ...\n\npres.writeFile({ fileName: "./presentation.pptx" });\n```\n\n**使用子代理时 / If using subagents**：每张幻灯片一个 JS 文件存于 `slides/`，各自导出 `createSlide(pres, theme)`，用 `compile.js` 合并。\n\n### 第五步 · QA 检查 / Step 5 — QA Checklist\n\n生成后运行，确认无误再交付。\nRun after generation. Fix before declaring success.\n\n**内容 QA / Content QA**:\n```bash\npython -m markitdown ./presentation.pptx\npython -m markitdown ./presentation.pptx | grep -iE "xxxx|lorem|ipsum|placeholder"\n```\n\n**质量清单 / Quality checklist**:\n- [ ] 结论优先结构：能用一句话说出演示的核心信息？/ Conclusion-first: state the key message in 1 sentence?\n- [ ] 每张幻灯片只有 1 个断言标题（不是话题标签）/ Each slide has exactly 1 assertion heading\n- [ ] 标题字数：中文 ≤ 15 字，英文 ≤ 6 词；超出必须精简，不允许换行 / Title length: CN ≤15 chars, EN ≤6 words — rewrite if over, never wrap\n- [ ] 每张内容页至少 1 个非文字视觉元素（图表、图标、形状）/ Every content slide has ≥1 non-text visual element\n- [ ] 除封面外所有幻灯片有页码徽章 / Page number badges on all slides except cover\n- [ ] 字号：标题 36-44pt，正文 14-16pt，说明 10-12pt / Font sizes: title 36-44pt, body 14-16pt, captions 10-12pt\n- [ ] 颜色只用选定配色方案，十六进制无 `#` 前缀 / Colors only from chosen palette; no `#` prefix\n- [ ] 不用 Unicode 子弹符号（`•`），用 PptxGenJS `bullet: true` / No unicode bullets\n- [ ] 没有 `shadow.offset` < 0（会损坏文件）/ No `shadow.offset` < 0\n- [ ] PptxGenJS 调用之间不复用 options 对象 / No reused option objects across calls\n- [ ] 所有元素均在页面边界内：`x + w ≤ 10`，`y + h ≤ 5.625`；流程图/时间轴已用命名变量推导坐标 / All elements within bounds; process-flow & timeline coords derived from named variables\n\n\n---\n\n## 路径三 · 编辑模板 / Path 3 — Editing Templates\n\n**完整流程见 [editing.md](references/editing.md)。**\n**Read [editing.md](references/editing.md) for full details.**\n\n快速流程 / Quick workflow:\n1. 分析 · Analyze: `markitdown`\n2. 解包 · Unpack: `scripts/office/unpack.py`\n3. 规划幻灯片映射（布局多样化）/ Plan slide mapping (varied layouts)\n4. 结构变更（删除/复制/排序）/ Structural changes (delete/duplicate/reorder)\n5. 编辑内容 — **每个幻灯片 XML 并行使用子代理** / Edit content — **use subagents in parallel** per slide XML\n6. **替换所有表格和图表数据**（见下方关键说明）/ **Replace ALL table and chart data** (see below)\n7. 清理 · Clean: `scripts/clean.py`\n8. 打包 · Pack: `scripts/office/pack.py`\n9. 验证 · Validate: `scripts/office/validate.py`（XML 结构）+ `markitdown` grep（残留占位符）\n\n### 关键：模板数据替换 / Critical: Template Data Replacement\n\n**所有前代 skill 都在这里失败了。** 当编辑含有**表格**（`<a:tbl>`）或**图表**（`ppt/charts/`）的模板时，必须：\nAll previous skills failed here. When editing a template containing **tables** or **charts**, you MUST:\n\n**第一步 / Step 1 — 清查 / Inventory**:\n```bash\ngrep -r "a:tbl" ./unpacked/ppt/slides/ --include="*.xml" -l\nls ./unpacked/ppt/charts/\n```\n\n**第二步 / Step 2 — 替换表格数据 / Replace table data**，每个单元格的 `<a:t>` 内容都要替换：\n```xml\n<!-- 模板原始数据 / Template data -->\n<a:tc><a:txBody><a:p><a:r><a:t>Steaming time</a:t></a:r></a:p></a:txBody></a:tc>\n<!-- 替换为实际内容 / Replace with actual data -->\n<a:tc><a:txBody><a:p><a:r><a:t>Q1 Revenue</a:t></a:r></a:p></a:txBody></a:tc>\n```\n用 **Edit 工具**逐个替换，不用 sed/python。/ Use **Edit tool**, not sed/python.\n\n**第三步 / Step 3 — 替换图表数据 / Replace chart data**，编辑 `./unpacked/ppt/charts/chartN.xml` 中的 `<c:ser>` 数据：\n```xml\n<c:cat><c:strRef><c:strCache>\n  <c:pt idx="0"><c:v>YOUR_LABEL_1</c:v></c:pt>\n</c:strCache></c:strRef></c:cat>\n<c:val><c:numRef><c:numCache>\n  <c:pt idx="0"><c:v>42</c:v></c:pt>\n</c:numCache></c:numRef></c:val>\n```\n\n**第四步 / Step 4 — 验证替换 / Verify replacement**:\n```bash\npython -m markitdown ./output.pptx | grep -i "steaming\\|tapai\\|lorem\\|xxxx"\n```\n有任何匹配 = 模板数据未完全替换，修复后再交付。\nAny match = template data not fully replaced. Fix before declaring success.\n\n---\n\n## 设计建议 / Design Ideas\n\n**不要制作无聊的幻灯片。** 白底黑字加项目符号不会打动任何人。\n**Don\'t create boring slides.** Plain bullets on a white background won\'t impress anyone.\n\n### 开始前 / Before Starting\n\n- **选择大胆、与内容匹配的配色方案** — 如果把你的颜色换到完全不同的话题上还能用，说明选得不够具体。/ **Pick a bold, content-informed palette** — if it still "works" for a different topic, you haven\'t been specific enough.\n- **主色支配原则**：一种颜色占 60-70% 视觉比重，1-2 个辅助色，一个强调色。/ **Dominance over equality**: one color dominates (60-70%), 1-2 supporting tones, one sharp accent.\n- **深浅对比**：标题页和结尾页用深色背景，内容页用浅色（"三明治"结构）。/ **Dark/light contrast**: dark for title + closing, light for content ("sandwich" structure).\n- **坚持一种视觉母题**：选定一个独特元素并在所有幻灯片中重复 — 圆形图片框、彩色圆圈图标、单侧粗边框。/ **Commit to a visual motif**: ONE distinctive element repeated across all slides.\n\n### 每张幻灯片的布局选项 / Layout Options per Slide\n\n- 两列（文字在左，视觉在右）/ Two-column (text left, visual right)\n- 图标 + 文字行（彩色圆圈图标，加粗标题，下方说明）/ Icon + text rows\n- 2×2 或 2×3 网格 / 2×2 or 2×3 grid\n- 半出血图片加内容叠层 / Half-bleed image with content overlay\n- 大数字标注（60-72pt 数字加小标签）/ Large stat callouts (60-72pt numbers)\n- 对比列（前后、优缺点）/ Comparison columns\n- 时间线 / 流程图（编号步骤、箭头）/ Timeline / process flow\n\n### 避免的常见错误 / Avoid (Common Mistakes)\n\n- **相邻幻灯片不用相同布局** / Never repeat the same layout across adjacent slides\n- **正文左对齐，不要居中** / Never center body text\n- **不要默认用蓝色** — 选择专为这个主题设计的颜色 / Never default to blue\n- **标题下不加装饰线** — AI 生成幻灯片的标志性特征 / Never use accent lines under titles\n- **不做纯文字幻灯片** — 添加图标、形状或图表 / Never text-only slides\n- **不用低对比度元素** — 图标和文字都要与背景有强对比 / Never low-contrast elements\n\n---\n\n## 依赖 / Dependencies\n\n```bash\n# Python（必须 / Required）\npip install defusedxml lxml Pillow "markitdown[pptx]"\n\n# Node.js（从零创建时必须 / Required for creating from SkillObservation(content='Base directory for this skill: /workspace/app-bu7wnuu44l4x/.skills/docx\n\n# DOCX creation, editing, and analysis\n\n## Overview\n\nA user may ask you to create, edit, or analyze the contents of a .docx file. A .docx file is essentially a ZIP archive containing XML files and other resources that you can read or edit. You have different tools and workflows available for different tasks.\n\n## Workflow Decision Tree\n\n### Reading/Analyzing Content\nUse "Text extraction" or "Raw XML access" sections below\n\n### Creating New Document\nUse "Creating a new Word document" workflow\n\n### Editing Existing Document\n- **Your own document + simple changes**\n  Use "Basic OOXML editing" workflow\n\n- **Someone else\'s document**\n  Use **"Redlining workflow"** (recommended default)\n\n- **Legal, academic, business, or government docs**\n  Use **"Redlining workflow"** (required)\n\n## Reading and analyzing content\n\n### Text extraction\nIf you just need to read the text contents of a document, you should convert the document to markdown using pandoc. Pandoc provides excellent support for preserving document structure and can show tracked changes:\n\n```bash\n# Convert document to markdown with tracked changes\npandoc --track-changes=all path-to-file.docx -o output.md\n# Options: --track-changes=accept/reject/all\n```\n\n### Raw XML access\nYou need raw XML access for: comments, complex formatting, document structure, embedded media, and metadata. For any of these features, you\'ll need to unpack a document and read its raw XML contents.\n\n#### Unpacking a file\n`python ooxml/scripts/unpack.py <office_file> <output_directory>`\n\n#### Key file structures\n* `word/document.xml` - Main document contents\n* `word/comments.xml` - Comments referenced in document.xml\n* `word/media/` - Embedded images and media files\n* Tracked changes use `<w:ins>` (insertions) and `<w:del>` (deletions) tags\n\n## Creating a new Word document\n\nWhen creating a new Word document from scratch, use **docx-js**, which allows you to create Word documents using JavaScript/TypeScript.\n\n### Workflow\n1. **MANDATORY - READ ENTIRE FILE**: Read [`docx-js.md`](docx-js.md) (~500 lines) completely from start to finish. **NEVER set any range limits when reading this file.** Read the full file content for detailed syntax, critical formatting rules, and best practices before proceeding with document creation.\n2. Create a JavaScript/TypeScript file using Document, Paragraph, TextRun components (You can assume all dependencies are installed, but if not, refer to the dependencies section below)\n3. Export as .docx using Packer.toBuffer()\n\n## Editing an existing Word document\n\nWhen editing an existing Word document, use the **Document library** (a Python library for OOXML manipulation). The library automatically handles infrastructure setup and provides methods for document manipulation. For complex scenarios, you can access the underlying DOM directly through the library.\n\n### Workflow\n1. **MANDATORY - READ ENTIRE FILE**: Read [`ooxml.md`](ooxml.md) (~600 lines) completely from start to finish. **NEVER set any range limits when reading this file.** Read the full file content for the Document library API and XML patterns for directly editing document files.\n2. Unpack the document: `python ooxml/scripts/unpack.py <office_file> <output_directory>`\n3. Create and run a Python script using the Document library (see "Document Library" section in ooxml.md)\n4. Pack the final document: `python ooxml/scripts/pack.py <input_directory> <office_file>`\n\nThe Document library provides both high-level methods for common operations and direct DOM access for complex scenarios.\n\n## Redlining workflow for document review\n\nThis workflow allows you to plan comprehensive tracked changes using markdown before implementing them in OOXML. **CRITICAL**: For complete tracked changes, you must implement ALL changes systematically.\n\n**Batching Strategy**: Group related changes into batches of 3-10 changes. This makes debugging manageable while maintaining efficiency. Test each batch before moving to the next.\n\n**Principle: Minimal, Precise Edits**\nWhen implementing tracked changes, only mark text that actually changes. Repeating unchanged text makes edits harder to review and appears unprofessional. Break replacements into: [unchanged text] + [deletion] + [insertion] + [unchanged text]. Preserve the original run\'s RSID for unchanged text by extracting the `<w:r>` element from the original and reusing it.\n\nExample - Changing "30 days" to "60 days" in a sentence:\n```python\n# BAD - Replaces entire sentence\n\'<w:del><w:r><w:delText>The term is 30 days.</w:delText></w:r></w:del><w:ins><w:r><w:t>The term is 60 days.</w:t></w:r></w:ins>\'\n\n# GOOD - Only marks what changed, preserves original <w:r> for unchanged text\n\'<w:r w:rsidR="00AB12CD"><w:t>The term is </w:t></w:r><w:del><w:r><w:delText>30</w:delText></w:r></w:del><w:ins><w:r><w:t>60</w:t></w:r></w:ins><w:r w:rsidR="00AB12CD"><w:t> days.</w:t></w:r>\'\n```\n\n### Tracked changes workflow\n\n1. **Get markdown representation**: Convert document to markdown with tracked changes preserved:\n   ```bash\n   pandoc --track-changes=all path-to-file.docx -o current.md\n   ```\n\n2. **Identify and group changes**: Review the document and identify ALL changes needed, organizing them into logical batches:\n\n   **Location methods** (for finding changes in XML):\n   - Section/heading numbers (e.g., "Section 3.2", "Article IV")\n   - Paragraph identifiers if numbered\n   - Grep patterns with unique surrounding text\n   - Document structure (e.g., "first paragraph", "signature block")\n   - **DO NOT use markdown line numbers** - they don\'t map to XML structure\n\n   **Batch organization** (group 3-10 related changes per batch):\n   - By section: "Batch 1: Section 2 amendments", "Batch 2: Section 5 updates"\n   - By type: "Batch 1: Date corrections", "Batch 2: Party name changes"\n   - By complexity: Start with simple text replacements, then tackle complex structural changes\n   - Sequential: "Batch 1: Pages 1-3", "Batch 2: Pages 4-6"\n\n3. **Read documentation and unpack**:\n   - **MANDATORY - READ ENTIRE FILE**: Read [`ooxml.md`](ooxml.md) (~600 lines) completely from start to finish. **NEVER set any range limits when reading this file.** Pay special attention to the "Document Library" and "Tracked Change Patterns" sections.\n   - **Unpack the document**: `python ooxml/scripts/unpack.py <file.docx> <dir>`\n   - **Note the suggested RSID**: The unpack script will suggest an RSID to use for your tracked changes. Copy this RSID for use in step 4b.\n\n4. **Implement changes in batches**: Group changes logically (by section, by type, or by proximity) and implement them together in a single script. This approach:\n   - Makes debugging easier (smaller batch = easier to isolate errors)\n   - Allows incremental progress\n   - Maintains efficiency (batch size of 3-10 changes works well)\n\n   **Suggested batch groupings:**\n   - By document section (e.g., "Section 3 changes", "Definitions", "Termination clause")\n   - By change type (e.g., "Date changes", "Party name updates", "Legal term replacements")\n   - By proximity (e.g., "Changes on pages 1-3", "Changes in first half of document")\n\n   For each batch of related changes:\n\n   **a. Map text to XML**: Grep for text in `word/document.xml` to verify how text is split across `<w:r>` elements.\n\n   **b. Create and run script**: Use `get_node` to find nodes, implement changes, then `doc.save()`. See **"Document Library"** section in ooxml.md for patterns.\n\n   **Note**: Always grep `word/document.xml` immediately before writing a script to get current line numbers and verify text content. Line numbers change after each script run.\n\n5. **Pack the document**: After all batches are complete, convert the unpacked directory back to .docx:\n   ```bash\n   python ooxml/scripts/pack.py unpacked reviewed-document.docx\n   ```\n\n6. **Final verification**: Do a comprehensive check of the complete document:\n   - Convert final document to markdown:\n     ```bash\n     pandoc --track-changes=all reviewed-document.docx -o verification.md\n     ```\n   - Verify ALL changes were applied correctly:\n     ```bash\n     grep "original phrase" verification.md  # Should NOT find it\n     grep "replacement phrase" verification.md  # Should find it\n     ```\n   - Check that no unintended changes were introduced\n\n\n## Converting Documents to Images\n\nTo visually analyze Word documents, convert them to images using a two-step process:\n\n1. **Convert DOCX to PDF**:\n   ```bash\n   soffice --headless --convert-to pdf document.docx\n   ```\n\n2. **Convert PDF pages to JPEG images**:\n   ```bash\n   pdftoppm -jpeg -r 150 document.pdf page\n   ```\n   This creates files like `page-1.jpg`, `page-2.jpg`, etc.\n\nOptions:\n- `-r 150`: Sets resolution to 150 DPI (adjust for quality/size balance)\n- `-jpeg`: Output JPEG format (use `-png` for PNG if preferred)\n- `-f N`: First page to convert (e.g., `-f 2` starts from page 2)\n- `-l N`: Last page to convert (e.g., `-l 5` stops at page 5)\n- `page`: Prefix for output files\n\nExample for specific range:\n```bash\npdftoppm -jpeg -r 150 -f 2 -l 5 document.pdf page  # Converts only pages 2-5\n```\n\n## Code Style Guidelines\n**IMPORTANT**: When generating code for DOCX operations:\n- Write concise code\n- Avoid verbose variable names and redundant operations\n- Avoid unnecessary print statements\n\n## Dependencies\n\nRequired dependencies (install if not available):\n\n- **pandoc**: `sudo apt-get install pandoc` (for text extraction)\n- **docx**: `npm install -g docx` (for creating new documents)\n- **LibreOffice**: `sudo apt-get install libreoffice` (for PDF conversion)\n- **Poppler**: `sudo apt-get install poppler-utils` (for pdftoppm to convert PDF to images)\n- **defusedxml**: `pip install defusedxml` (for secure XML parsing)', observation=<ObservationType.SKILL: 'skill'>, skill='docx', success=True, data={'skill_path': '/workspace/miaoda_skills/prompts/system/docx/SKILL.md', 'scripts_path': '/workspace/miaoda_skills/prompts/system/docx/scripts'})

SkillObservation(content='Base directory for this skill: /workspace/app-bu7wnuu44l4x/.skills/pdf\n\n# PDF Processing Guide\n\n## Overview\n\nThis guide covers essential PDF processing operations using Python libraries and command-line tools. For advanced features, JavaScript libraries, and detailed examples, see reference.md. If you need to fill out a PDF form, read forms.md and follow its instructions.\n\n## Quick Start\n\n```python\nfrom pypdf import PdfReader, PdfWriter\n\n# Read a PDF\nreader = PdfReader("document.pdf")\nprint(f"Pages: {len(reader.pages)}")\n\n# Extract text\ntext = ""\nfor page in reader.pages:\n    text += page.extract_text()\n```\n\n## Python Libraries\n\n### pypdf - Basic Operations\n\n#### Merge PDFs\n```python\nfrom pypdf import PdfWriter, PdfReader\n\nwriter = PdfWriter()\nfor pdf_file in ["doc1.pdf", "doc2.pdf", "doc3.pdf"]:\n    reader = PdfReader(pdf_file)\n    for page in reader.pages:\n        writer.add_page(page)\n\nwith open("merged.pdf", "wb") as output:\n    writer.write(output)\n```\n\n#### Split PDF\n```python\nreader = PdfReader("input.pdf")\nfor i, page in enumerate(reader.pages):\n    writer = PdfWriter()\n    writer.add_page(page)\n    with open(f"page_{i+1}.pdf", "wb") as output:\n        writer.write(output)\n```\n\n#### Extract Metadata\n```python\nreader = PdfReader("document.pdf")\nmeta = reader.metadata\nprint(f"Title: {meta.title}")\nprint(f"Author: {meta.author}")\nprint(f"Subject: {meta.subject}")\nprint(f"Creator: {meta.creator}")\n```\n\n#### Rotate Pages\n```python\nreader = PdfReader("input.pdf")\nwriter = PdfWriter()\n\npage = reader.pages[0]\npage.rotate(90)  # Rotate 90 degrees clockwise\nwriter.add_page(page)\n\nwith open("rotated.pdf", "wb") as output:\n    writer.write(output)\n```\n\n### pdfplumber - Text and Table Extraction\n\n#### Extract Text with Layout\n```python\nimport pdfplumber\n\nwith pdfplumber.open("document.pdf") as pdf:\n    for page in pdf.pages:\n        text = page.extract_text()\n        print(text)\n```\n\n#### Extract Tables\n```python\nwith pdfplumber.open("document.pdf") as pdf:\n    for i, page in enumerate(pdf.pages):\n        tables = page.extract_tables()\n        for j, table in enumerate(tables):\n            print(f"Table {j+1} on page {i+1}:")\n            for row in table:\n                print(row)\n```\n\n#### Advanced Table Extraction\n```python\nimport pandas as pd\n\nwith pdfplumber.open("document.pdf") as pdf:\n    all_tables = []\n    for page in pdf.pages:\n        tables = page.extract_tables()\n        for table in tables:\n            if table:  # Check if table is not empty\n                df = pd.DataFrame(table[1:], columns=table[0])\n                all_tables.append(df)\n\n# Combine all tables\nif all_tables:\n    combined_df = pd.concat(all_tables, ignore_index=True)\n    combined_df.to_excel("extracted_tables.xlsx", index=False)\n```\n\n### reportlab - Create PDFs\n\n#### Basic PDF Creation\n```python\nfrom reportlab.lib.pagesizes import letter\nfrom reportlab.pdfgen import canvas\n\nc = canvas.Canvas("hello.pdf", pagesize=letter)\nwidth, height = letter\n\n# Add text\nc.drawString(100, height - styles[\'Normal\']))\n\n# Build PDF\ndoc.build(story)\n```\n\n## Command-Line Tools\n\n### pdftotext (poppler-utils)\n```bash\n# Extract text\npdftotext input.pdf output.txt\n\n# Extract text - 是否附加图片摘要\n * @param options.withLinksSummary - 是否附加链接汇总\n * @param options.targetSelector - CSS 选择器，仅提取特定元素\n * @param options.removeSelector - CSS 选择器，移除特定元素\n * @param options.timeout - "https://deno.land/std/http/server.ts";\n\nserve(async (req: Request): Promise<Response> => {\n  /**\n   * Web Reader Edge Function\n   * 接收前端请求，注入平台密钥后转发至上游 Jina Reader 服务。\n   * 请求体：{ url: string, options?: {...} }\n   * 响应：{ content: string }\n   */\n  if (req.method !== "POST") {\n    return new Response("Method Not Allowed", { status: 405 });\n  }\n\n  // --- 解析客户端请求 ---\n  let targetUrl: string;\n  let returnFormat: string | undefined;\n  let withImagesSummary: boolean | undefined;\n  let withLinksSummary: boolean | undefined;\n  let targetSelector: string | undefined;\n  let removeSelector: string | undefined;\n  let timeout: number | undefined;\n  let noCache: boolean | undefined;\n\n  try {\n    const body = await req.json();\n    targetUrl = body.url;\n    if (!targetUrl) throw new Error("Missing url");\n    returnFormat = body.returnFormat;\n    withImagesSummary = body.withImagesSummary;\n    withLinksSummary = body.withLinksSummary;\n    targetSelector = body.targetSelector;\n    removeSelector = body.removeSelector;\n    timeout = body.timeout;\n    noCache = body.noCache;\n  } catch {\n    return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: "Invalid request body" }), {\n      status: 400,\n      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },\n    });\n  }\n\n  // --- 注入平台密钥（严禁暴露至前端） ---\n  const apiKey = Deno.env.get("INTEGRATIONS_API_KEY");\n  if (!apiKey) {\n    return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: "Server configuration error" }), {\n      status: 500,\n      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },\n    });\n  }\n\n  // --- 构造请求头 ---\n  const upstreamHeaders: Record<string, string> = {\n    "X-Gateway-Authorization": `Bearer ${apiKey}`,\n  };\n  if (returnFormat) upstreamHeaders["X-Return-Format"] = returnFormat;\n  if (withImagesSummary !== undefined) {\n    upstreamHeaders["X-With-Images-Summary"] = String(withImagesSummary);\n  }\n  if (withLinksSummary !== undefined) {\n    upstreamHeaders["X-With-Links-Summary"] = String(withLinksSummary);\n  }\n  if (targetSelector) upstreamHeaders["X-Target-Selector"] = targetSelector;\n  if (removeSelector) upstreamHeaders["X-Remove-Selector"] = removeSelector;\n  if (timeout !== undefined) upstreamHeaders["X-Timeout"] = String(timeout);\n  if (noCache) upstreamHeaders["X-No-Cache"] = "true";\n\n  // --- 调用上游服务 ---\n  const encodedUrl = encodeURIComponent(targetUrl);\n  const upstream = await fetch(\n    `https://app-bu7wnuu44l4x-api-ELbWqODdAgNY-gateway.appmiaoda.com/${encodedUrl}`,\n    { method: "GET", headers: upstreamHeaders }\n  );\n\n  // 转发鉴权/过滤错误\n  if (upstream.status === 401 || upstream.status === 403) {\n    const errText = await upstream.text();\n    return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: errText || `Upstream error: ${upstream.status}` }), {\n      status: upstream.status,\n      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },\n    });\n  }\n\n  // 转发配额/余额错误\n  if (upstream.status === 429 || upstream.status === 402) {\n    const errText = await upstream.text();\n    return new Response(errText, {\n      status: upstream.status,\n      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },\n    });\n  }\n\n  if (!upstream.ok) {\n    return new Response(\n      JSON.stringify({ error: `Upstream error: ${upstream.status}` }),\n      { status: 502, headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" } }\n    );\n  }\n\n  const content = await upstream.text();\n  return new Response(JSON.stringify({ content }), {\n    status: 200,\n    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },\n  });\n});\n```\n\n### 前端调用代码（Web / MiniProgram 通用）\n\n**推荐方式（supabase client 可用时）：**\n\n```typescript\n/**\n * 通过 Edge Function 抓取网页内容。\n * @param url - 目标网页完整 URL\n * @param options - 可选的输出控制参数\n * @returns 网页 Markdown 文本\n */\nasync function fetchWebPage(\n  url: string,\n  options?: {\n    returnFormat?: "markdown" | "html" | "text" | "screenshot" | "pageshot";\n    withImagesSummary?: boolean;\n    withLinksSummary?: boolean;\n    targetSelector?: string;\n    removeSelector?: string;\n    timeout?: number;\n    noCache?: boolean;\n  }\n): Promise<string> {\n  const { data, error } = await supabase.functions.invoke("web-reader", {\n    body: { url, ...options },\n  });\n  if (error) throw error;\n  if (!data?.content) throw new Error("返回内容为空");\n  return data.content;\n}\n```\n\n**备用方式（无法使用 supabase client 时）：**\n\n```typescript\n/**\n * 通过原生 fetch 调用 Edge Function 抓取网页内容。\n * @param url - 目标网页完整 URL\n * @param options - 可选的输出控制参数\n * @returns 网页 Markdown 文本\n */\nasync function fetchWebPage(\n  url: string,\n  options?: {\n    returnFormat?: "markdown" | "html" | "text";\n    withImagesSummary?: boolean;\n    withLinksSummary?: boolean;\n    targetSelector?: string;\n    removeSelector?: string;\n    timeout?: number;\n    noCache?: boolean;\n  }\n): Promise<string> {\n  const res = await fetch(`${import.meta.env.VITE_SUPABASE_URL}/functions/v1/web-reader`, {\n    method: "POST",\n    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },\n    body: JSON.stringify({ url, ...options }),\n  });\n\n  if (res.status === 429) {\n    const err = await res.json();\n    throw new Error(`配额已用尽：${err.message ?? res.statusText}`);\n  }\n  if (res.status === 402) {\n    const err = await res.json();\n    throw new Error(`余额不足：${err.message ?? res.statusText}`);\n  }\n  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`请求失败：${res.status}`);\n\n  const json = await res.json();\n  if (!json.content) throw new Error("返回内容为空");\n  return json.content;\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 参数说明\n\n### 请求参数\n\n| 参数名 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |\n|--------|------|------|------|\n| `url` | string | 是 | 目标网页的完整 URL，含 scheme（如 `https://example.com`） |\n| `returnFormat` | string | 否 | 返回格式：`markdown`（默认）/ `html` / `text` / `screenshot` / `pageshot` |\n| `withImagesSummary` | boolean | 否 | 是否在内容中附加图片描述摘要 |\n| `withLinksSummary` | boolean | 否 | 是否在内容末尾附加链接汇总 |\n| `targetSelector` | string | 否 | CSS 选择器，仅提取页面特定元素内容 |\n| `removeSelector` | string | 否 | CSS 选择器，从结果中移除特定元素 |\n| `timeout` | number | 否 | 等待页面加载的超时时间（秒） |\n| `noCache` | boolean | 否 | 设为 `true` 禁用缓存，强制重新抓取 |\n| `accept` | string | 否 | 请求头 `Accept` 值：`text/html`（默认）或 `text/event-stream`（开启 SSE 流式模式） |\n\n### 返回字段说明\n\n| 字段路径 | 类型 | 说明 |\n|----------|------|------|\n| `content` | string | 目标网页的结构化 Markdown 文本，包含标题、正文及可选的图片摘要、链接列表 |\n\n---\n\n## 注意事项\n\n- **密钥安全**：`INTEGRATIONS_API_KEY` 仅可在 Edge Function 服务端读取，严禁暴露到前端。\n- **错误处理**：务必处理 401（Token 无效）、403（GFW 过滤）、429（配额超限）和 402（余额不足）。\n- **计费**：本插件免费（`original_price: 0.00`，`enable_billing: false`），但仍会统计调用次数。\n- **GFW 过滤**：JWT Payload 中的 `filter_gfw` 字段控制是否过滤 GFW 屏蔽域名，被过滤的 URL 将返回 403。\n- **URL 编码**：目标 URL 需经过 `encodeURIComponent` 编码后再拼接到路径中，避免特殊字符导致路由解析错误。\n- **响应格式**：默认返回 `text/plain` 格式的 Markdown，Edge Function 已将其包装为 `{ content: string }` JSON，方便前端直接使用。\n- **流式响应**：如需流式接收内容，可在请求头中设置 `Accept: text/event-stream`，SSE 流式处理实现见下方"SSE 流式处理"章节。\n\n---\n\n## SSE 流式处理\n\n当请求头设置 `Accept: text/event-stream` 时，上游服务以 SSE（Server-Sent Events）格式流式返回内容，每个 SSE 事件携带部分 Markdown 文本。流式模式适用于大页面实时渲染场景。\n\n### SSE 事件格式\n\n```\ndata: <部分 Markdown 文本>\n\ndata: <更多 Markdown 文本>\n\ndata: [DONE]\n\n```\n\n- 每条 `data:` 行携带一段内容片段。\n- 收到 `data: [DONE]` 表示流结束。\n\n### 生成期 SSE 流式调用示例\n\n```typescript\nconst apiKey = process.env["INTEGRATIONS_API_KEY"]!;\n\n/**\n * 以 SSE 流式模式抓取目标网页，实时处理返回的 Markdown 片段。\n * @param targetUrl - 目标网页的完整 URL\n * @param onChunk - 每收到一段内容时的回调函数\n * @returns 完整 Markdown 文本\n */\nasync function fetchWebPageSSE(\n  targetUrl: string,\n  onChunk?: (chunk: string) => void\n): Promise<string> {\n  const encodedUrl = encodeURIComponent(targetUrl);\n  const endpoint =\n    `https://app-bu7wnuu44l4x-api-ELbWqODdAgNY-gateway.appmiaoda.com/${encodedUrl}`;\n\n  const response = await fetch(endpoint, {\n    method: "GET",\n    headers: {\n      "X-Gateway-Authorization": `Bearer ${apiKey}`,\n      "Accept": "text/event-stream",\n    },\n  });\n\n  if (response.status === 401) throw new Error("鉴权失败：JWT Token 缺失或无效");\n  if (response.status Promise<VideoTask>\n): Promise<VideoResult> {\n  /**\n   * 提交视频生成任务，轮询直到完成或超时。\n   * @param submitFn 提交任务的函数，返回 { taskId }\n   * @returns 任务结果，含视频 URL 列表\n   */\n  const { taskId } = await submitFn();\n\n  const POLL_INTERVAL_MS = 7000;        // 每 7 秒轮询一次\n  const TIMEOUT_MS = 10 * 60 * 1000;   // 最长等待 10 分钟\n  const deadline = Date.now() + TIMEOUT_MS;\n\n  while (Date.now() < deadline) {\n    await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, POLL_INTERVAL_MS));\n\n    const queryResp = await fetch(\n queryResp.json();\n    if (queryJson.code !== 0) throw new Error(`API 错误 ${queryJson.code}：${queryJson.message}`);\n\n    const { task_status, task_result } = queryJson.data;\n\n    if (task_status === "succeed") {\n      return {\n        taskId,\n        status: "succeed",\n        videos: task_result?.videos?.map((v: { id: string; url: string; watermark_url: string; duration: string }) => ({\n          id: v.id,\n          url: v.url,\n          watermarkUrl: v.watermark_url,\n          duration: v.duration,\n        })),\n      };\n    }\n\n    if (task_status === "failed") {\n      throw new Error(`任务失败 (taskId: ${taskId})`);\n    }\n\n    // submitted / processing → 继续轮询\n  }\n\n  throw new Error(`任务 ${taskId} 超时（等待超过 10 分钟）`);\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 生成期用法（Agent 直接调用）\n\n使用平台注入的    159     setAttachments(prev => [...prev, ...valid.map(f => ({ name: f.name, type: f.type, size: f.size }))]);
   160     if (fileRef.current) fileRef.current.value = '';
   161   };
   162 
   163   const removeAttachment = (idx: number) => {
   164     setAttachments(prev => prev.filter((_, i) => i !== idx));
   165   };
   166 
   167   const getFileIcon = (type: string) => {
   168     if (type.startsWith('image/')) return <ImageIcon className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-sky-500" />;
   169     if (type.startsWith('video/')) return <Film className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-violet-500" />;
   170     return <FileUp className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-500" />;
   171   };
   172 
   173   const formatBytes = (b: number) => b < 1024 * 1024 ? `${(b / 1024).toFixed(1)}KB` : `${(b / 1024 / 1024).toFixed(1)}MB`;
   174 
   175   const toggleResourceType = (value: string, available: boolean) => {
   176     if (!available || generating) return;
   177     setResourceTypes(prev =>
   178       prev.includes(value)
   179         ? prev.length === 1 ? prev  // 至少保留一个
   180           : prev.filter(t => t !== value)
   181         : [...prev, value]
   182     );
   183   };
   184 
   185   const handleGenerate = async () => {
   186     if (!topic.trim()) { toast.error('请填写资源主题'); return; }
   187     if (!user) { toast.error('请先登录'); return; }
   188     if (resourceTypes.length === 0) { toast.error('请至少选择一种资源类型'); return; }
   189 
   190     setGenerating(true);
   191     setProgress(0);
   192     setCurrentStep(0);
   193     setLogs([`开始生成 ${resourceTypes.length} 种资源...`]);
   194     setResult(null);
   195 
   196     const stepInterval = setInterval(() => {
   197       setCurrentStep(prev => {
   198         if (prev >= agentSteps.length - 1) { clearInterval(stepInterval); return prev; }
   199         setLogs(l => [...l, `${agentSteps[prev + 1].label} 进行中...`]);
   200         return prev + 1;
   201       });
   202       setProgress(prev => Math.min(prev + Math.floor(85 / resourceTypes.length / agentSteps.length * agentSteps.length), 85));
   203     }, 1200);
   204 
   205     try {
   206       const results: { type: string; content: string }[] = [];
   207 
   208       for (let i = 0; i < resourceTypes.length; i++) {
   209         const rType = resourceTypes[i];
   210         const typeLabel = resourceTypeOptions.find(t => t.value === rType)?.label || rType;
   211         setLogs(l => [...l, `正在生成「${typeLabel}」（${i + 1}/${resourceTypes.length}）...`]);
   212 
   213         const data = await generateResource({
   214           course_name: '',
   215           topic,
   216           resource_type: rType,
   217           major: profile?.major,
   218           education: profile?.education,
   219         });
   220 
   221         results.push({ type: rType, content: data.content });
   222 
   223         await supabase.from('resources').insert({
   224           user_id: user.id,
   225           title: `${topic} ${typeLabel}`,
   226           description: `基于AI生成的${typeLabel}资源`,
   227           type: rType,
   228           content: data.content,
   229           status: 'completed',
   230           course_name: '',
   231           topic,
   232           resource_type: rType,
   233         });
   234 
   235         setProgress(Math.round(((i + 1) / resourceTypes.length) * 100));
   236       }
   237 
   238       clearInterval(stepInterval);
   239       setProgress(100);
   240       setCurrentStep(agentSteps.length - 1);
   241       setLogs(prev => [...prev, `全部 ${resourceTypes.length} 种资源生成完成！`]);
   242       // 展示第一种资源的结果
   243       setResult(results[0]?.content || null);
   244       toast.success(resourceTypes.length > 1 ? `成功生成 ${resourceTypes.length} 种资源！` : '资源生成成功！');
   245     } catch (err) {
   246       clearInterval(stepInterval);
   247       toast.error('生成失败：' + (err as Error).message);
   248       setLogs(prev => [...prev, `生成失败：${(err as Error).message}`]);
   249     } finally {
   250       setGenerating(false);

   101 function StageNode({
   102   stage, index, isCurrent, isCompleted, isLocked,
   103   onComplete, completing, onDelete,
   104   onDragStart, onDragOver, onDrop,
   105   isDragging, activeTool,
   106 }: {
   107   stage: PathStage; index: number; isCurrent: boolean; isCompleted: boolean; isLocked: boolean;
   108   onComplete: () => void; completing: boolean; onDelete: () => void;
   109   onDragStart: (e: React.DragEvent) => void;
   110   onDragOver: (e: React.DragEvent) => void;
   111   onDrop: (e: React.DragEvent) => void;
   112   isDragging?: boolean;
   113   activeTool?: string;
   114 }) {
   115   const theme = STAGE_THEMES[index % STAGE_THEMES.length];
   116   const [expanded, setExpanded] = useState(true);
   117 
   118   return (
   119     <div
   120       draggable={activeTool === 'select' || !activeTool}
   121       onDragStart={onDragStart}
   122       onDragOver={onDragOver}
   123       onDrop={onDrop}

   125       style={{ width: 256 }}
   126     >
   127       <motion.div
   128         initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
   129         animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
   130         transition={{ delay: index * 0.06, type: 'spring', stiffness: 260, damping: 22 }}
   131         className={`relative bg-card rounded-2xl border-2 shadow-lg transition-all duration-200 select-none group
   132           ${isCompleted ? 'border-primary/30 opacity-80' : isCurrent ? `border-transparent ring-2 ${theme.ring} ${theme.glow} shadow-xl` : 'border-border opacity-70'}
   133           ${isLocked ? 'cursor-not-allowed' : activeTool === 'delete' ? 'cursor-pointer hover:border-destructive/60 hover:shadow-destructive/20' : 'cursor-grab active:cursor-grabbing hover:shadow-xl hover:-translate-y-0.5'}
   134           ${isDragging ? 'opacity-70 scale-105 z-50 shadow-2xl' : ''}
   135         `}
   136       >
   137         {/* 顶部色带 */}
   138         <div className={`h-1.5 rounded-t-2xl bg-gradient-to-r ${theme.from} ${theme.to}`} />

   145               {isCompleted

   149             <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
   150               <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-balance leading-tight">{stage.title}</h3>

     1 import { supabase } from '@/db/supabase';
     2 import { createParser } from 'eventsource-parser';
     3 
     4 export interface ChatMessage {
     5   role: 'user' | 'assistant' | 'system';
     6   content: string;
     7 }
     8 
     9 export interface StreamCallbacks {
    10   onChunk: (text: string) => void;
    11   onDone: () => void;
    12   onError: (error: string) => void;
    13 }
    14 
    15 export async function streamChat(
    16   messages: ChatMessage[],
    17   callbacks: StreamCallbacks,
    18   promptType: 'tutoring' | 'portrait' | 'resource' = 'tutoring',
    19   signal?: AbortSignal,
    20   systemPrompt?: string
    21 ) {
    22   try {
    23     const response = await supabase.functions.invoke<ReadableStream>('ai-chat', {
    24       method: 'POST',
    25       body: { messages, prompt_type: promptType, system_prompt: systemPrompt },
    26     });
    27 
    28     if (response.error) {
    29       const errorMsg = await response.error?.context?.text?.() || response.error.message;
    30       callbacks.onError(errorMsg);
    31       return;
    32     }
    33 
    34     // 由于 Supabase functions.invoke 返回的是 parsed data，我们改用 fetch 直接调用
    35   } catch {
    36     // 回退到直接 fetch
    37   }
    38 
    39   // 直接 fetch 调用 Edge Function 获取 SSE 流
    40   const url = `${import.meta.env.VITE_SUPABASE_URL}/functions/v1/ai-chat`;
    41   const resp = await fetch(url, {
    42     method: 'POST',
    43     headers: {
    44       'Content-Type': 'application/json',
    45       'Authorization': `Bearer ${import.meta.env.VITE_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY}`,
    46     },
    47     body: JSON.stringify({ messages, prompt_type: promptType, system_prompt: systemPrompt }),
    48     signal,
    49   });
    50 
    51   if (!resp.ok || !resp.body) {
    52     callbacks.onError(`请求失败: ${resp.status}`);
    53     return;
    54   }
    55 
    56   const reader = resp.body.getReader();
    57   const decoder = new TextDecoder('utf8');
    58   const parser = createParser({
    59     onEvent: (event) => {
    60       if (!event.data || event.data === '[DONE]') return;
    61       try {
    62         const chunk = JSON.parse(event.data);
    63         const content = chunk.choices?.[0]?.delta?.content ?? '';
    64         if (content) callbacks.onChunk(content);
    65       } catch {
    66         // skip malformed
    67       }
    68     },
    69   });
    70 
    71   try {
    72     while (true) {
    73       const { done, value } = await reader.read();
    74       if (done) break;
    75       parser.feed(decoder.decode(value, { stream: true }));
    76     }
    77     callbacks.onDone();
    78   } catch (err) {
    79     if ((err as Error).name === 'AbortError') return;
    80     callbacks.onError((err as Error).message);
    81   }
    82 }
    83 
    84 export async function generateResource(
    85   params: {
    86     course_name: string;
    87     topic: string;
    88     resource_type: string;
    89     major?: string;
    90     education?: string;
    91   }
    92 ): Promise<{ content: string; type: string }> {
    93   const { data, error } = await supabase.functions.invoke<{ content: string; type: string }>('ai-generate', {
    94     method: 'POST',
    95     body: params,
    96   });
    97 
    98   if (error) {
    99     const errorMsg = await error?.context?.text?.() || error.message;
   100     throw new Error(errorMsg);
   101   }
   102 
   103   if (!data) throw new Error('未返回数据');
   104 
   105   // 净化内容：去除所有 * 符号（Markdown 加粗/列表），转为结构化纯文本
   106   const cleanContent = (raw: string) => {
   107     return raw
   108       .replace(/\*{1,3}([^*]+)\*{1,3}/g, '$1') // 去除 **bold** / *italic* / ***bold-italic***
   109       .replace(/^\s*\*+\s+/gm, '• ')            // 将 * 列表项替换为 • 符号
   110       .replace(/^\s*#{1,6}\s+/gm, '')           // 可选：去除 markdown 标题符号（保留文字）
   111       .replace(/\n{3,}/g, '\n\n')               // 合并多余空行
   112       .trim();
   113   };
   114 
   115   return { ...data, content: cleanContent(data.content) };
   116 }

   265             {/* 邀请记录 */}
   266             <Card>
   267               <CardHeader className="pb-3">
   268                 <CardTitle className="text-base flex items-center gap-2">
   269                   <Users className="w-4 h-4 text-primary" />
   270                   邀请记录
   271                   {inviteRecords.length > 0 && (
   272                     <Badge variant="secondary" className="ml-auto text-xs">{inviteRecords.length} 位好友</Badge>
   273                   )}
   274                 </CardTitle>
   275               </CardHeader>
   276               <CardContent>
   277                 {inviteRecords.length === 0 ? (
   278                   <div className="text-center py-8 text-muted-foreground">
   279                     <Users className="w-10 h-10 mx-auto mb-2 opacity-30" />
   280                     <p className="text-sm">还没有邀请记录，快去分享吧！</p>
   281                   </div>
   282                 ) : (
   283                   <div className="space-y-2">
   284                     {inviteRecords.map((rec, i) => (
   285                       <div key={rec.id} className="flex items-center gap-3 px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-muted/50">
   286                         <div className="w-7 h-7 rounded-full bg-primary/10 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 text-xs font-bold text-primary">
   287                           {i + 1}
   288                         </div>
   289                         <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
   290                           <p className="text-sm font-medium">好友 {rec.invitee_id.slice(0, 8).toUpperCase()}</p>
   291                           <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{new Date(rec.created_at).toLocaleDateString('zh-CN')}</p>
   292                         </div>
   293                         <Badge className="bg-amber-100 text-amber-700 dark:bg-amber-900/60 dark:text-amber-300 border-0 text-xs shrink-0">
   294                           +50 积分
   295                         </Badge>
   296                       </div>
   297                     ))}
   298                   </div>
   299                 )}
   300               </CardContent>
   301             </Card>
   302           </TabsContent>
   303 
   304           {/* ─── Tab: 积分记录 ─────────────────────────────── */}
   305           <TabsContent value="points" className="mt-4 space-y-4">
   306             {/* 积分余额卡 */}
   307             <div className="relative overflow-hidden rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-br from-violet-500 to-purple-600 p-5 text-white shadow-md">
   308               <div className="absolute -right-6 -top-6 w-28 h-28 rounded-full bg-white/10" />
   309               <p className="text-sm font-medium opacity-90 mb-1 relative">当前积分余额</p>
   310               <p className="text-5xl font-black relative">{balance}</p>
   311               <p className="text-xs opacity-75 mt-1 relative">积分可兑换套餐 · 越多越划算</p>
   312             </div>

   315             <Card>

   318               </CardHeader>
   319               <CardContent>
   320                 <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-2">

   323                     { label: '每日签到', pts: '+5',  icon: '📅' },

   330                       <p className="text-sm font-bold text-primary">{item.pts}</p>
   331                     </div>
   332                   ))}

   334               </CardContent>
   335             </Card>
   336

   338             <Card>
   339               <CardHeader className="pb-3">
   340                 <CardTitle className="text-base flex items-center gap-2">
   341                   <Coins className="w-4 h-4 text-primary" />
   342                   积分流水明细
   343                 </CardTitle>
   344               </CardHeader>
   345               <CardContent>
   346                 {transactions.length === 0 ? (
   347                   <div className="text-center py-8 text-muted-foreground">
   348                     <Coins className="w-10 h-10 mx-auto mb-2 opacity-30" />
   349                     <p className="text-sm">暂无积分记录，邀请好友或完成任务开始积累吧！</p>
   350                   </div>
   351                 ) : (
   352                   <div className="space-y-1.5">
   353                     {transactions.map(tx => (
   354                       <div key={tx.id} className="flex items-center gap-3 px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg hover:bg-muted/50 transition-colors">
   355                         <div className={`w-7 h-7 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 ${tx.delta > 0 ? 'bg-emerald-100 dark:bg-emerald-900/40' : 'bg-rose-100 dark:bg-rose-900/40'}`}>
   356                           {tx.delta > 0
   357                             ? <ArrowUpRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400" />
   358                             : <ArrowDownRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-rose-600 dark:text-rose-400" />
   359                           }
   360                         </div>
   361                         <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
   362                           <p className="text-sm font-medium">{ACTION_LABELS[tx.action] ?? tx.action}</p>
   363                           {tx.note && <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground truncate">{tx.note}</p>}
   364                           <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{new Date(tx.created_at).toLocaleString('zh-CN')}</p>
   365                         </div>
   366                         <span className={`text-sm font-bold shrink-0 ${tx.delta > 0 ? 'text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400' : 'text-rose-600 dark:text-rose-400'}`}>
   367                           {tx.delta > 0 ? `+${tx.delta}` : tx.delta}
   368                         </span>
   369                       </div>
   370                     ))}
   371                   </div>
   372                 )}
   373               </CardContent>
   374             </Card>
   375           </TabsContent>
   376 
   377           {/* ─── Tab: 升级套餐 ─────────────────────────────── */}
   378           <TabsContent value="plans" className="mt-4">
   379             <PlansTabContent plans={plans} currentPlanId={currentPlanId} upgradePlan={handleUpgrade} />
   380           </TabsContent>
   381         </Tabs>
   382         )}
   383       </div>
   384     </AppLayout>
   385   );
   386 }
   387 
   388 // ─── 套餐内容子组件（plansOnly时直接渲染，无需 TabsContent 包裹） ──────
   389 function PlansTabContent({
   390   plans, currentPlanId, upgradePlan,
   391 }: {
   392   plans: Plan[];
   393   currentPlanId: string;
   394   upgradePlan: (planId: string) => void;
   395 }) {
   396   const handleUpgrade = (planId: string) => {
   397     if (planId === currentPlanId) return;
   398     upgradePlan(planId);
   399   };
   400   const defaultPlans: Plan[] = [
   401     { id: 'free',       name: '免费版', price_month: 0,  price_year: 0,   features: ['10次/月资源生成', '基础学习路径', '社区支持'], sort_order: 1 },
   402     { id: 'basic',      name: '基础版', price_month: 19, price_year: 168, features: ['50次/月资源生成', '个性化路径', '答疑中心', '工单支持'], sort_order: 2 },
   403     { id: 'pro',        name: '高级版', price_month: 49, price_year: 428, features: ['200次/月资源生成', '高级AI路径', 'AI深度答疑', '全部工具', '优先支持'], sort_order: 3 },
   404     { id: 'enterprise', name: '专业版', price_month: 99, price_year: 828, features: ['无限资源生成', '专属顾问', '优先答疑', '全部工具', '团队协作', '数据导出', '专属客服'], sort_order: 4 },
   405   ];
   406   const displayPlans = plans.length > 0 ? plans : defaultPlans;
   407   return (
   408     <div className="space-y-5">
   409       {/* banner */}

   416           </div>
   417           <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-1 text-balance">选择最适合你的套餐</h2>
   418           <p className="text-white/75 text-sm">年付更优惠 · 随时可取消 · 7天无理由退款</p>
   419         </div>
   420       </div>
   421       <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-3">
   422         {displayPlans.map((plan, i) => {

   429                 {isCurrent && <div className="absolute top-3 right-3 z-10"><Badge className="bg-primary text-primary-foreground text-[10px] px-1.5 py-0.5">当前</Badge></div>}
   430                 {plan.id === 'pro' && !isCurrent && <div className="absolute top-0 left-0 right-0 h-0.5 bg-gradient-to-r from-violet-500 to-purple-500" />}
   431                 <div className={`p-4 bg-gradient-to-br ${meta.gradient}`}>
   432                   <div className={`w-9 h-9 rounded-xl ${meta.badge} flex items-center justify-center mb-2.5`}><PlanIcon className="w-4 h-4" /></div>
   433                   <p className="font-bold text-sm mb-1.5">{plan.name}</p>

     8	<Payment System>
     9	Required Items:
    10	1. Orders must be associated with the purchaser. If the scenario does not require login to complete payment, use the phone number as the purchaser ID
    11	2. Frontend purchase button → Invoke create_wechat_payment → Navigate to the order details page.
    12	3. Design an order details page that displays different content based on the order status:
    13	Pending: Display payment QR code. Poll order status every 2s until it is no longer pending, silently without any user-facing indication.
    14	Paid: Display corresponding functions (e.g., refund button)
    15	4. Edge functions: create_payment_order, wechat_payment_webhook
    16	5. create_wechat_payment must:
    17	  - Validate purchaser info
    18	  - Calculate total
    19	  - Generate WeChat Native payment URL
    20	  - Save to order
    21	  - return order_no
    22	6. wechat_payment_webhook must:
    23	  - Decrypt WeChat callback + verify payment
    24	  - Update order status using optimistic locking
    25	  - Trigger post-payment logic only once after successful update
    26	7. Inventory changes should be atomic RPCs.
    27	8. Secrets must follow exact names: MERCHANT_ID, MERCHANT_APP_ID, MCH_CERT_SERIAL_NO, MCH_PRIVATE_KEY, WECHAT_PAY_PUBLIC_KEY_ID, WECHAT_PAY_PUBLIC_KEY, MCH_API_V3_KEY. Notify users via Plugin Center.
    28	9. If key missing or createWechatPayUrl fails → frontend prompts user to check keys.
    29	10. Utility functions (createWechatPayUrl, decryptTradeState) must be used unmodified.
    30	```
    31	import { Wechatpay } from 'npm:wechatpay-axios-plugin';
    32	async function createWechatPayUrl(MERCHANT_ID, MERCHANT_APP_ID, MCH_CERT_SERIAL_NO, MCH_PRIVATE_KEY, WECHAT_PAY_PUBLIC_KEY_ID， WECHAT_PAY_PUBLIC_KEY, outTradeNo, amount, notifyUrl) {
    33	  try {
    34	    const wxpay = new Wechatpay({
    35	      mchid: MERCHANT_ID,
    36	      serial: MCH_CERT_SERIAL_NO,
    37	      privateKey: MCH_PRIVATE_KEY,
    38	      certs: {[WECHAT_PAY_PUBLIC_KEY_ID]: WECHAT_PAY_PUBLIC_KEY}
    39	    });
    40	    const res = await wxpay.v3.pay.transactions.native.post({
    41	      mchid: MERCHANT_ID,
    42	      out_trade_no: outTradeNo,
    43	      appid: MERCHANT_APP_ID,
    44	      description: '调用用途描述',
    45	      notify_url: notifyUrl,
    46	      amount: { total: Math.round(amount * 100) }
    47	    }, { headers: { 'Wechatpay-Serial': WECHAT_PAY_PUBLIC_KEY_ID } });
    48	    if (res.data.code_url) {
    49	      console.log(`[WeChatPay SUCCESS] outTradeNo=${outTradeNo}, url=${res.data.code_url}`);
    50	      return { success: true, url: res.data.code_url };
    51	    } else {
    52	      console.error(`[WeChatPay FAILED] outTradeNo=${outTradeNo}, error=${res.data.message || JSON.stringify(data)}`);
    53	      return { success: false, error: res.data.message || JSON.stringify(res.data) };
    54	    }
    55	  } catch (err) {
    56	    console.error(`[WeChatPay ERROR] outTradeNo=${outTradeNo}, error=${err?.message || String(err)}`);
    57	    return { success: false, error: err?.message || String(err) };
    58	  }
    59	}
    60	
    61	import { Aes } from "npm:wechatpay-axios-plugin";
    62	async function decryptTradeState(MCH_API_V3_KEY: string, associatedData: string, nonce: string, ciphertext: string): Promise<string> {
    63	  const plaintext = await Aes.AesGcm.decrypt(ciphertext, apiV3Key, nonce, aad);
    64	  const obj = JSON.parse(plaintext);
    65	  return {
    66	    status: (obj.trade_state ?? "").toString() === "SUCCESS" ? "SUCCESS" : "OTHERS",
    67	    order_no: obj.out_trade_no ?? ""
    68	  };
    69	}
    70	```

    78	  const yymmdd = new Date().toISOString().slice(2, 10).replace(/-/g, "");
    79	  return `ORD-${yymmdd}-${uid.rnd()}`;
    80	}

   113	    created_at timestamptz default now(),
   114	    updated_at timestamptz default now()
   115	);
   116	-- 订单明细表
   117	create table public.order_items (
   118	    id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
   119	    order_id uuid not null references public.orders(id) on delete cascade,
   120	    sku_code text not null references public.sku(sku_code),
   121	    quantity int not null check (quantity > 0),
   122	    unit_price numeric(10,2) not null,
   123	    total_price numeric(12,2) not null,
   124	    sku_snapshot jsonb not null,
   125	    created_at timestamptz default now()
   126	);
   127	
   128	create or replace function public.manage_sku_inventory(
   129	  sku_code_in text,
   130	  qty_in int,
   131	  action text  -- 'order' | 'pay_success' | 'pay_cancel'
   132	) returns sku
   133	language plpgsql
   134	as $$
   135	declare
   136	  result sku;
   137	begin
   138	  if qty_in is null or qty_in <= 0 then
   139	    result := null;
   140	    return result;
   141	  end if;
   142	
   143	  if action = 'order' then
   144	    -- 下单冻结：可售减少，冻结增加
   145	    update sku
   146	    set inventory_available = inventory_available - qty_in,
   147	        inventory_reserved = inventory_reserved + qty_in,
   148	        updated_at = now()
   149	    where sku_code = sku_code_in
   150	      and inventory_available >= qty_in
   151	    returning * into result;
   152	
   153	  elsif action = 'pay_success' then
   154	    -- 支付成功：冻结转已售
   155	    update sku
   156	    set inventory_reserved = inventory_reserved - qty_in,
   157	        inventory_sold = inventory_sold + qty_in,
   158	        updated_at = now()
   159	    where sku_code = sku_code_in
   160	      and inventory_reserved >= qty_in
   161	    returning * into result;
   162	
   163	  elsif action = 'pay_cancel' then
   164	    -- 支付取消/超时：释放冻结回可售
   165	    update sku
   166	    set inventory_available = inventory_available + qty_in,
   167	        inventory_reserved = inventory_reserved - qty_in,
   168	        updated_at = now()
   169	    where sku_code = sku_code_in
   170	      and inventory_reserved >= qty_in
   171	    returning * into result;
   172	  elsif action = 'refund' then
   173	    -- 支付成功后退款：释放已售回可售
   174	    update sku
   175	    set inventory_available = inventory_available + qty_in,
   176	        inventory_sold = inventory_sold - qty_in,
   177	        updated_at = now()
   178	    where sku_code = sku_code_in
   179	      and inventory_sold >= qty_in
   180	    returning * into result;
   181	  else
   182	    result := null;
   183	  end if;
   184	
   185	  return result;
   186	end;
   187	$$;
   188	-- If users don't need the concept of inventory, set the default stock to 999999.
   189	```
   190	</Payment System>
   191	</Refund System>

   233	    return {success: true, data};

   243	import { Aes } from "npm:wechatpay-axios-plugin";
   244	async function decryptRefundState(MCH_API_V3_KEY: string, associatedData: string, nonce: string, ciphertext: string): Promise<{ refundStatus: string; outTradeNo: string; outRefundNo: string }> {
   245	  const plaintext = await Aes.AesGcm.decrypt(ciphertext, MCH_API_V3_KEY, nonce, associatedData);
   246	  const obj = JSON.parse(plaintext);
   247	  return {
   248	    refundStatus: (obj.refund_status ?? "").toString() === "SUCCESS" ? "SUCCESS" : "OTHERS",
   249	    outTradeNo: obj.out_trade_no ?? "",
   250	    outRefundNo: obj.out_refund_no ?? ""
   251	  };
   252	}
